The code provided has several vulnerabilities and incorrect error handling. Here's a detailed analysis:

1. **Incorrect Handling of Buffer Underrun**: When `write(fd, buf, remain)` returns 0, indicating a buffer underrun, the function breaks out of the loop but does not account for the bytes that were successfully written. This leads to an incorrect return value, as the function returns `len - remain` without considering the partial write.

2. **Invalid File Descriptor Handling**: The function does not check if `fd` is a valid file descriptor. Passing an invalid descriptor to `write()` can lead to undefined behavior or crashes.

3. **Incomplete Error Handling for EINR**: The function checks for `EINTR` and `EAGAIN` when `ret < 0`, but it does not handle `EINR` (input/output error, resource unavailable). This can result in incorrect error codes being returned.
